gammabench

Verified snapshots · explicit work contracts · paired comparisons

Measurement CSV · Comparison CSV · Unsupported / censored CSV · Analysis JSON · Report checksums

How to read these results
  • Historical imports retain original evidence; these values are not new reruns.
  • Observed ranges describe the captured samples, not confidence intervals.
  • Profile scopes may cover more work than completion metrics; inclusive costs are not additive phases.

Summary medians use completed measurement samples. Warmups are excluded. Timeouts and missing samples remain visible and suppress pair rankings. Ratios are candidate / baseline; speedup above 1 favours the candidate using the metric's declared direction. Ratios require exact semantic qualification, equal contracts and metric scopes, and complete matching rounds.
